Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Idea:
MP-Pack-Ensure: add the missing 'actually install' verb so vibecomfy can chain install-plan → install → reload in one command, plus pin ComfyUI-QwenTTS in the lockfile.

Today (post-P1) the discovery and verification machinery is wired but installation itself is a printed plan, not an action:
- vibecomfy/commands/nodes.py has _cmd_nodes_install_plan() which scans a workflow, finds missing class_types, looks them up in KNOWN_NODE_PACKS, and PRINTS the suggested packs.
- vibecomfy/node_packs.py:KNOWN_NODE_PACKS is the static registry mapping class_type → CustomNodePack(name, repo, classes, pip_packages).
- vibecomfy/node_packs_lockfile.py reads/writes custom_nodes.lock with git_commit_sha pins (added in P1).
- vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py fail-closes on SHA drift before session.start() (added in P1).
- EmbeddedSession.reload_for_nodepack_change() is the post-install reload verb (added in P1).

The gap: no command actually performs the install. User has to copy the printed git clone + pip install commands and run them by hand. This is also why ComfyUI-QwenTTS is referenced in ready_templates/video/ltx2_3_runexx_talking_avatar_qwen_tts.py:363 but is not pinned in custom_nodes.lock — the workflow worked with manual install but never went through the lockfile path.

DELIVERABLES:

1. New command 'vibecomfy nodes install <pack-name>' OR '--repo <url>' that:
   - Clones the repo into the local custom_nodes directory (search the project for the canonical install path; check vibecomfy/runtime/session.py and existing scripts to find where Comfy expects custom nodes — likely a 'custom_nodes/' dir).
   - Pip-installs the pack's pip_packages from KNOWN_NODE_PACKS (use subprocess with the project's existing pip command discovery; mirror what install-plan currently SUGGESTS).
   - On success, calls into vibecomfy/node_packs_lockfile.py to UPSERT the new entry with git_commit_sha read from the cloned repo HEAD.
   - On failure (clone error, pip error), prints a clear error and exits non-zero. Does NOT mutate the lockfile on failure.
   - Idempotent: if the repo already exists at the install path with a clean working tree, just refreshes the SHA and returns success. If dirty, refuses unless --force.

2. New command 'vibecomfy nodes ensure --template <template-id>' OR '--workflow <path>' that:
   - Calls the existing install-plan logic to identify missing packs.
   - For each missing pack, calls the new 'install' verb.
   - After all installs, optionally calls session.reload_for_nodepack_change() if a session is reachable; if not, prints clearly that the user should reload/restart their session.
   - Exits non-zero if any install fails.
   - Has --dry-run that just prints the plan (essentially aliasing install-plan for ergonomics).

3. Pin ComfyUI-QwenTTS in custom_nodes.lock by:
   - Adding the QwenTTS entry to vibecomfy/node_packs.py:KNOWN_NODE_PACKS (lookup the actual class_types it provides — at minimum 'AILab_Qwen3TTSVoiceClone' from the talking-avatar template; check the pack's repo for the full class list).
   - Running 'vibecomfy nodes install ComfyUI-QwenTTS' (or scripted equivalent at execution time) to populate the lockfile entry, OR if installing during the megaplan run is risky, just record the intended entry and document the user-action follow-up.
   - Repo URL: research the canonical ComfyUI-QwenTTS repo (likely under 'AIFSH' or similar org on GitHub — verify before committing).

4. Optional but nice-to-have: add an 'ensure_packs: bool = False' kwarg to EmbeddedSession.run(...) that calls the ensure path before executing. Default False (opt-in, no surprise installs). If True and a pack is missing, install + reload + then execute. Document explicitly that this is a convenience for dev workflows; production should pre-stage installs via 'vibecomfy nodes ensure'.

5. Tests:
   - Unit test for the install verb using a mocked subprocess (don't actually clone repos in CI). Assert: lockfile UPSERT happens on success, no mutation on failure, idempotent on existing clean clone.
   - Unit test for ensure that mocks install and asserts the dependency-resolution logic.
   - If ensure_packs kwarg is added: integration-style test (mocked) that asserts session.run with ensure_packs=True calls the install path before queue_prompt_api.

CRITICAL CONSTRAINTS:

- Backward compat: existing 'vibecomfy nodes install-plan' must keep working unchanged — install is a NEW verb, not a replacement.
- Do NOT actually clone real repos or pip-install during the megaplan execution phase — use mocks for tests. The user (or post-merge CI) can do real installs.
- Do NOT touch P1 territory beyond what's necessary: vibecomfy/commands/nodes.py and vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py are P1's; touching them to ADD new subcommands is fine, but don't refactor what P1 just shipped.
- Do NOT add hot-loading without session reload — sticking to the SD-005 / plan §'Custom Nodes — Adding On the Fly' policy: 'Hot-loading a new nodepack into an already-running HiddenSwitch executor is not the default. Adding a new pack defaults to session reload or restart.'
- Do NOT touch vibecomfy/blocks/, vibecomfy/workflow.py, vibecomfy/runtime/session.py beyond adding the optional ensure_packs kwarg surface (and only if cleanly additive).

KEY FILES:
- vibecomfy/commands/nodes.py (existing install-plan; new install + ensure subcommands here)
- vibecomfy/node_packs.py:KNOWN_NODE_PACKS (registry to extend with QwenTTS)
- vibecomfy/node_packs_lockfile.py (LockEntry, read_lockfile, write_lockfile — P1 just added these)
- vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py (existing SHA verification)
- vibecomfy/runtime/session.py (EmbeddedSession.reload_for_nodepack_change exists from P1; optionally add ensure_packs kwarg to .run())
- ready_templates/video/ltx2_3_runexx_talking_avatar_qwen_tts.py:363 (the template that uses QwenTTS)
- custom_nodes.lock (current entries: KJNodes, WanVideoWrapper)
- docs/python_composition_dsl_plan.md §'Custom Nodes' for design intent

ACCEPTANCE:
- 'vibecomfy nodes install ComfyUI-QwenTTS' (with appropriate args) installs the pack and updates the lockfile (mocked in tests; real install is user follow-up).
- 'vibecomfy nodes ensure --template video/ltx2_3_runexx_talking_avatar_qwen_tts' identifies missing packs and installs them (mocked).
- ComfyUI-QwenTTS is recorded in KNOWN_NODE_PACKS with its class types.
- Existing 'install-plan' command still works.
- All existing tests pass.
- New tests for install / ensure behavior pass with mocks.
- doctor still fail-closes on a synthetic mismatch.

The plan is explicit: --force on a dirty checkout skips the clone but does NOT run git checkout or git reset. It just UPSERTs the current HEAD. This prevents accidental coupling and keeps the runtime startup cost unchanged when the kwarg is not used.\n\nENSURE_PACKS RELOAD CONSERVATISM. Skip reload_for_nodepack_change when missing_packs_for_workflow returns empty. The fake-runner tests for both paths are required, not optional.\n\nDEBT WATCH. Don't be tempted to fix them here \u2014 this is MP-Pack-Ensure scope only.\n\nJUDGMENT CALL. The plan asks an open question about lockfile format (legacy vs TOML).         Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan places _coercion_log.py under vibecomfy/blocks/ but also needs vibeworkflow.connect in vibecomfy/workflow.py to consult handle_trace_active and record events. because importing vibecomfy.blocks._coercion_log executes vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py, and that package currently imports vibeworkflow at top level, any top-level import from workflow.py to vibecomfy.blocks._coercion_log would create a circular import risk unless the implementation uses carefully lazy imports or moves the shared trace module outside the blocks package. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure: does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the proposed _coercion_log.py under vibecomfy/blocks/ is supporting infrastructure that both blocks and workflow need. given the current package dependency direction (blocks imports workflow), the safer location is a neutral module such as vibecomfy/handles.py or vibecomfy/handle_warnings.py; leaving it under blocks increases import-order fragility unless the implementation is very deliberate. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them: find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: for the block helper caller path, vibeworkflow.connect is usually called by vibecomfy.blocks._utils.connect, not by the user's block function directly. the plan's immediate-caller-frame heuristic can work if _utils.connect has a handle local named source, but it will not identify the higher-level block function unless record_raw_ref_coercion preserves or walks to the block frame rather than skipping blocks frames. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] handle-equality-hash-semantics: strict python hash/eq invariant violated for the bare-id compatibility bridge (handle('1', 0) == '1' and == '1.0' but hash('1') != hash('1.0')). (flagged 2 times across 2 plans)
- [DEBT] handle-equality-hash-semantics: hash/eq compromise is documented but not strictly satisfied for bare-id bridge. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] import-architecture: import architecture: placing shared trace state in `vibecomfy.blocks._coercion_log` risks circular imports because `workflow.py` needs the state for connect-site detection while `vibecomfy.blocks.__init__` currently imports `vibeworkflow` at package import time. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] search-for-related-code-that-handles-the-same-concept-is-the-reported-issue-a-symptom-of-something-broader: search for related code that handles the same concept. is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: the plan's record_raw_ref_coercion helper says it finds the first non-vibecomfy/blocks frame for the site. that conflicts with the stated goal that raw string refs in block code are visible: a missed handles(image="...") inside a block called by a ready template could be attributed to an allowlisted ready_templates frame and suppressed, or at minimum would not identify the block module as the source. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] untyped-raw-ref-doctor-rule-coverage: runtime detection covers handles values and connect refs but not arbitrary python dict keys outside those flows. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] wf-node-out-api-contract: non-digit slot names raise notimplementederror instead of returning a handle; diverges from brief's .out(name_or_index) literal wording. (flagged 2 times across 2 plans)
- [DEBT] wf-node-out-api-contract: same as issue_hints-1. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

        Idea:
MP-Pack-Ensure: add the missing 'actually install' verb so vibecomfy can chain install-plan → install → reload in one command, plus pin ComfyUI-QwenTTS in the lockfile.

Today (post-P1) the discovery and verification machinery is wired but installation itself is a printed plan, not an action:
- vibecomfy/commands/nodes.py has _cmd_nodes_install_plan() which scans a workflow, finds missing class_types, looks them up in KNOWN_NODE_PACKS, and PRINTS the suggested packs.
- vibecomfy/node_packs.py:KNOWN_NODE_PACKS is the static registry mapping class_type → CustomNodePack(name, repo, classes, pip_packages).
- vibecomfy/node_packs_lockfile.py reads/writes custom_nodes.lock with git_commit_sha pins (added in P1).
- vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py fail-closes on SHA drift before session.start() (added in P1).
- EmbeddedSession.reload_for_nodepack_change() is the post-install reload verb (added in P1).

The gap: no command actually performs the install. User has to copy the printed git clone + pip install commands and run them by hand. This is also why ComfyUI-QwenTTS is referenced in ready_templates/video/ltx2_3_runexx_talking_avatar_qwen_tts.py:363 but is not pinned in custom_nodes.lock — the workflow worked with manual install but never went through the lockfile path.

DELIVERABLES:

1. New command 'vibecomfy nodes install <pack-name>' OR '--repo <url>' that:
   - Clones the repo into the local custom_nodes directory (search the project for the canonical install path; check vibecomfy/runtime/session.py and existing scripts to find where Comfy expects custom nodes — likely a 'custom_nodes/' dir).
   - Pip-installs the pack's pip_packages from KNOWN_NODE_PACKS (use subprocess with the project's existing pip command discovery; mirror what install-plan currently SUGGESTS).
   - On success, calls into vibecomfy/node_packs_lockfile.py to UPSERT the new entry with git_commit_sha read from the cloned repo HEAD.
   - On failure (clone error, pip error), prints a clear error and exits non-zero. Does NOT mutate the lockfile on failure.
   - Idempotent: if the repo already exists at the install path with a clean working tree, just refreshes the SHA and returns success. If dirty, refuses unless --force.

2. New command 'vibecomfy nodes ensure --template <template-id>' OR '--workflow <path>' that:
   - Calls the existing install-plan logic to identify missing packs.
   - For each missing pack, calls the new 'install' verb.
   - After all installs, optionally calls session.reload_for_nodepack_change() if a session is reachable; if not, prints clearly that the user should reload/restart their session.
   - Exits non-zero if any install fails.
   - Has --dry-run that just prints the plan (essentially aliasing install-plan for ergonomics).

3. Pin ComfyUI-QwenTTS in custom_nodes.lock by:
   - Adding the QwenTTS entry to vibecomfy/node_packs.py:KNOWN_NODE_PACKS (lookup the actual class_types it provides — at minimum 'AILab_Qwen3TTSVoiceClone' from the talking-avatar template; check the pack's repo for the full class list).
   - Running 'vibecomfy nodes install ComfyUI-QwenTTS' (or scripted equivalent at execution time) to populate the lockfile entry, OR if installing during the megaplan run is risky, just record the intended entry and document the user-action follow-up.
   - Repo URL: research the canonical ComfyUI-QwenTTS repo (likely under 'AIFSH' or similar org on GitHub — verify before committing).

4. Optional but nice-to-have: add an 'ensure_packs: bool = False' kwarg to EmbeddedSession.run(...) that calls the ensure path before executing. Default False (opt-in, no surprise installs). If True and a pack is missing, install + reload + then execute. Document explicitly that this is a convenience for dev workflows; production should pre-stage installs via 'vibecomfy nodes ensure'.

5. Tests:
   - Unit test for the install verb using a mocked subprocess (don't actually clone repos in CI). Assert: lockfile UPSERT happens on success, no mutation on failure, idempotent on existing clean clone.
   - Unit test for ensure that mocks install and asserts the dependency-resolution logic.
   - If ensure_packs kwarg is added: integration-style test (mocked) that asserts session.run with ensure_packs=True calls the install path before queue_prompt_api.

CRITICAL CONSTRAINTS:

- Backward compat: existing 'vibecomfy nodes install-plan' must keep working unchanged — install is a NEW verb, not a replacement.
- Do NOT actually clone real repos or pip-install during the megaplan execution phase — use mocks for tests. The user (or post-merge CI) can do real installs.
- Do NOT touch P1 territory beyond what's necessary: vibecomfy/commands/nodes.py and vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py are P1's; touching them to ADD new subcommands is fine, but don't refactor what P1 just shipped.
- Do NOT add hot-loading without session reload — sticking to the SD-005 / plan §'Custom Nodes — Adding On the Fly' policy: 'Hot-loading a new nodepack into an already-running HiddenSwitch executor is not the default. Adding a new pack defaults to session reload or restart.'
- Do NOT touch vibecomfy/blocks/, vibecomfy/workflow.py, vibecomfy/runtime/session.py beyond adding the optional ensure_packs kwarg surface (and only if cleanly additive).

KEY FILES:
- vibecomfy/commands/nodes.py (existing install-plan; new install + ensure subcommands here)
- vibecomfy/node_packs.py:KNOWN_NODE_PACKS (registry to extend with QwenTTS)
- vibecomfy/node_packs_lockfile.py (LockEntry, read_lockfile, write_lockfile — P1 just added these)
- vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py (existing SHA verification)
- vibecomfy/runtime/session.py (EmbeddedSession.reload_for_nodepack_change exists from P1; optionally add ensure_packs kwarg to .run())
- ready_templates/video/ltx2_3_runexx_talking_avatar_qwen_tts.py:363 (the template that uses QwenTTS)
- custom_nodes.lock (current entries: KJNodes, WanVideoWrapper)
- docs/python_composition_dsl_plan.md §'Custom Nodes' for design intent

ACCEPTANCE:
- 'vibecomfy nodes install ComfyUI-QwenTTS' (with appropriate args) installs the pack and updates the lockfile (mocked in tests; real install is user follow-up).
- 'vibecomfy nodes ensure --template video/ltx2_3_runexx_talking_avatar_qwen_tts' identifies missing packs and installs them (mocked).
- ComfyUI-QwenTTS is recorded in KNOWN_NODE_PACKS with its class types.
- Existing 'install-plan' command still works.
- All existing tests pass.
- New tests for install / ensure behavior pass with mocks.
- doctor still fail-closes on a synthetic mismatch.

The CLI module is print + exit-code translation only.\n\nORDERING. Strict execution order: T1 (UPSERT helper) \u2192 T2 (neutral module) \u2192 T3 (neutral module tests) \u2192 T4 (CLI wrappers + refactor install-plan) \u2192 T5 (CLI tests) \u2192 T6 (ensure_packs kwarg) \u2192 T7 (runtime tests) \u2192 T8 (QwenTTS pin + docs) \u2192 T9 (full sweep). T4 and T6 both depend on T2 but are independent of each other; T8 depends on T4 because the docs reference the new install verb.\n\nINSTALL-PLAN REFACTOR \u2014 ZERO BEHAVIOR CHANGE. The extraction of resolve logic into missing_packs_for_workflow is pure plumbing. Print formatting, exit codes, and --json shape MUST remain byte-identical. Run tests/test_cli.py::test_nodes_install_plan_suggests_pack_for_missing_class immediately after the refactor as the canary \u2014 if it fails the extraction is wrong.\n\nSUBPROCESS INJECTION. install_pack accepts `runner: Callable = subprocess.run` as a parameter. Tests pass a fake; production uses the default. Do NOT use module-global monkeypatching \u2014 it's the brittle pattern this plan explicitly avoids. The fake records call signatures and returns either CompletedProcess(stdout=..., stderr=..., returncode=0) or raises CalledProcessError, depending on which test path you're exercising.\n\nDIRTY + FORCE = NO DESTRUCTIVE OPS. The plan is explicit: --force on a dirty checkout skips the clone but does NOT run git checkout or git reset. It just UPSERTs the current HEAD. This is conservative on purpose \u2014 destructive ops require explicit user request.\n\nQWENTTS PIN MECHANICS. Run `git ls-remote https://github.com/1038lab/ComfyUI-QwenTTS.git HEAD` (read-only network, no clone, no pip \u2014 this is allowed by the no-real-clone constraint). Capture the SHA from stdout, then edit custom_nodes.lock. Pick the lower-friction format: if the file is still 2 legacy lines, append a third legacy line; if migrating to TOML is simpler given the existing P1 _cmd_nodes_lock writes TOML, do that. Both are read-compatible per read_lockfile. Verify KNOWN_NODE_PACKS at vibecomfy/node_packs.py:116 \u2014 the plan says QwenTTS is already there with AILab_Qwen3TTSVoiceClone; if it is, no code edit. If it is not, add it.\n\nENSURE_PACKS LAZY IMPORT. The import at the top of vibecomfy/runtime/session.py must NOT include vibecomfy.node_packs_install. Put the import inside the run() body, conditionally on ensure_packs=True. This prevents accidental coupling and keeps the runtime startup cost unchanged when the kwarg is not used.\n\nENSURE_PACKS RELOAD CONSERVATISM. Skip reload_for_nodepack_change when missing_packs_for_workflow returns empty. The brief and SD-005 are conservative: only reload when a pack was actually installed or refreshed.\n\nLOCKFILE NON-MUTATION ON FAILURE. This is the most important invariant in the install_pack logic. Two failure paths (clone, pip) \u2014 both must leave custom_nodes.lock untouched. The fake-runner tests for both paths are required, not optional.\n\nDEBT WATCH. The pre-existing debt items (handle hash/eq, blocks\u2192workflow circular import risk, .out(name) NotImplementedError) are tracked but UNRELATED to this PR. Don't be tempted to fix them here \u2014 this is MP-Pack-Ensure scope only.\n\nJUDGMENT CALL. The plan asks an open question about lockfile format (legacy vs TOML). - [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan places _coercion_log.py under vibecomfy/blocks/ but also needs vibeworkflow.connect in vibecomfy/workflow.py to consult handle_trace_active and record events. because importing vibecomfy.blocks._coercion_log executes vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py, and that package currently imports vibeworkflow at top level, any top-level import from workflow.py to vibecomfy.blocks._coercion_log would create a circular import risk unless the implementation uses carefully lazy imports or moves the shared trace module outside the blocks package. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure: does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the proposed _coercion_log.py under vibecomfy/blocks/ is supporting infrastructure that both blocks and workflow need. given the current package dependency direction (blocks imports workflow), the safer location is a neutral module such as vibecomfy/handles.py or vibecomfy/handle_warnings.py; leaving it under blocks increases import-order fragility unless the implementation is very deliberate. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them: find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: for the block helper caller path, vibeworkflow.connect is usually called by vibecomfy.blocks._utils.connect, not by the user's block function directly. the plan's immediate-caller-frame heuristic can work if _utils.connect has a handle local named source, but it will not identify the higher-level block function unless record_raw_ref_coercion preserves or walks to the block frame rather than skipping blocks frames. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

...\n+\n+\n+@dataclass(frozen=True)\n+class InstallResult:\n+    name: str\n+    status: InstallStatus\n+    git_commit_sha: str | None\n+    error: str | None\n+\n+\n+def install_pack(\n+    *,\n+    name: str | None = None,\n+    repo: str | None = None,\n+    force: bool = False,\n+    install_root: Path = Path(\"custom_nodes\"),\n+    lockfile_path: Path = Path(\"custom_nodes.lock\"),\n+    runner: Runner = subprocess.run,\n+) -> InstallResult:\n+    if name is None and repo is None:\n+        raise ValueError(\"install_pack requires either name or repo\")\n+\n+    pack = _pack_by_name(name) if name is not None else None\n+    if pack is None and repo is None:\n+        raise ValueError(f\"unknown custom node pack {name!r}; pass repo to install an uncatalogued pack\")\n+\n+    pack_name = name or _pack_name_from_repo(repo or \"\")\n+    if not pack_name:\n+        raise ValueError(f\"could not infer custom node pack name from repo {repo!r}\")\n+    repo_url = repo or (pack.repo if pack is not None else None)\n+    if repo_url is None:\n+        raise ValueError(f\"missing repo URL for custom node pack {pack_name!r}\")\n+\n+    install_dir = install_root / pack_name\n+    if install_dir.exists():\n+        dirty = _git_porcelain(install_dir, runner)\n+        if dirty is None:\n+            return InstallResult(pack_name, \"failed\", None, f\"failed to inspect git status for {install_dir}\")\n+        if dirty and not force:\n+            return InstallResult(pack_name, \"skipped_dirty\", None, f\"{install_dir} has uncommitted changes; pass --force to refresh the lockfile pin\")\n+        sha = _git_head(install_dir, runner)\n+        if sha is None:\n+            return InstallResult(pack_name, \"failed\", None, f\"failed to read git HEAD for {install_dir}\")\n+        upsert_lockfile_entry(LockEntry(name=pack_name, git_commit_sha=sha, url=repo_url), lockfile_path)\n+        return InstallResult(pack_name, \"refreshed\", sha, None)\n+\n+    try:\n+        runner([\"git\", \"clone\", repo_url, str(install_dir)], check=True, capture_output=True, text=True)\n+    except (OSError, subprocess.CalledProcessError) as exc:\n+        return InstallResult(pack_name, \"failed\", None, _error_text(exc) or f\"failed to clone {repo_url}\")\n+\n+    pip_packages = pack.pip_packages if pack is not None else ()\n+    if pip_packages:\n+        try:\n+            runner(\n+                [sys.executable, \"-m\", \"pip\", \"install\", *pip_packages],\n+                check=True,\n+                capture_output=True,\n+                text=True,\n+            )\n+        except (OSError, subprocess.CalledProcessError) as exc:\n+            return InstallResult(pack_name, \"failed\", None, _error_text(exc) or \"failed to install pip packages\")\n+\n+    sha = _git_head(install_dir, runner)\n+    if sha is None:\n+        return InstallResult(pack_name, \"failed\", None, f\"failed to read git HEAD for {install_dir}\")\n+    upsert_lockfile_entry(LockEntry(name=pack_name, git_commit_sha=sha, url=repo_url), lockfile_path)\n+    return InstallResult(pack_name, \"installed\", sha, None)\n+\n+\n+def missing_packs_for_workflow(workflow: VibeWorkflow) -> tuple[list[CustomNodePack], list[str]]:\n+    class_types = {node.class_type for node in workflow.nodes.values()}\n+    missing_classes = class_types - _known_schema_classes()\n+    return resolve_node_packs(missing_classes), unresolved_class_types(missing_classes)\n+\n+\n+def _pack_by_name(name: str | None) -> CustomNodePack | None:\n+    if name is None:\n+        return None\n+    for pack in KNOWN_NODE_PACKS:\n+        if pack.name == name:\n+            return pack\n+    return None\n+\n+\n+def _pack_name_from_repo(repo: str) -> str:\n+    parsed = urlparse(repo)\n+    path = parsed.path or repo\n+    name = Path(path.rstrip(\"/\")).name\n+    if name.endswith(\".git\"):\n+        name = name[:-4]\n+    return name\n+\n+\n+def _git_porcelain(pack_dir: Path, runner: Runner) -> str | None:\n+    try:\n+        result = runner(\n+            [\"git\", \"-C\", str(pack_dir), \"status\", \"--porcelain\"],\n+            check=True,\n+            capture_output=True,\n+            text=True,\n+        )\n+    except (OSError, subprocess.CalledProcessError):\n+        return None\n+    return result.stdout\n+\n+\n+def _git_head(pack_dir: Path, runner: Runner) -> str | None:\n+    try:\n+        result = runner(\n+            [\"git\", \"-C\", str(pack_dir), \"rev-parse\", \"HEAD\"],\n+            check=True,\n+            capture_output=True,\n+            text=True,\n+        )\n+    except (OSError, subprocess.CalledProcessError):\n+        return None\n+    return result.stdout.strip() or None\n+\n+\n+def _known_schema_classes(path: Path = Path(\"node_index.json\")) -> set[str]:\n+    if not path.exists():\n+        return set()\n+    try:\n+        rows = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n+    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):\n+        return set()\n+    if not isinstance(rows, list):\n+        return set()\n+    return {str(row.get(\"class_type\")) for row in rows if isinstance(row, dict) and row.get(\"class_type\")}\n+\n+\n+def _error_text(exc: BaseException) -> str | None:\n+    stderr = getattr(exc, \"stderr\", None)\n+    if isinstance(stderr, bytes):\n+        stderr = stderr.decode(errors=\"replace\")\n+    if isinstance(stderr, str) and stderr.strip():\n+        return stderr.strip()\n+    stdout = getattr(exc, \"stdout\", None)\n+    if isinstance(stdout, bytes):\n+        stdout = stdout.decode(errors=\"replace\")\n+    if isinstance(stdout, str) and stdout.strip():\n+        return stdout.strip()\n+    return str(exc) or None\n*** End Patch\n"

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy

        Idea:
MP-Pack-Ensure: add the missing 'actually install' verb so vibecomfy can chain install-plan → install → reload in one command, plus pin ComfyUI-QwenTTS in the lockfile.

Today (post-P1) the discovery and verification machinery is wired but installation itself is a printed plan, not an action:
- vibecomfy/commands/nodes.py has _cmd_nodes_install_plan() which scans a workflow, finds missing class_types, looks them up in KNOWN_NODE_PACKS, and PRINTS the suggested packs.
- vibecomfy/node_packs.py:KNOWN_NODE_PACKS is the static registry mapping class_type → CustomNodePack(name, repo, classes, pip_packages).
- vibecomfy/node_packs_lockfile.py reads/writes custom_nodes.lock with git_commit_sha pins (added in P1).
- vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py fail-closes on SHA drift before session.start() (added in P1).
- EmbeddedSession.reload_for_nodepack_change() is the post-install reload verb (added in P1).

The gap: no command actually performs the install. User has to copy the printed git clone + pip install commands and run them by hand. This is also why ComfyUI-QwenTTS is referenced in ready_templates/video/ltx2_3_runexx_talking_avatar_qwen_tts.py:363 but is not pinned in custom_nodes.lock — the workflow worked with manual install but never went through the lockfile path.

DELIVERABLES:

1. New command 'vibecomfy nodes install <pack-name>' OR '--repo <url>' that:
   - Clones the repo into the local custom_nodes directory (search the project for the canonical install path; check vibecomfy/runtime/session.py and existing scripts to find where Comfy expects custom nodes — likely a 'custom_nodes/' dir).
   - Pip-installs the pack's pip_packages from KNOWN_NODE_PACKS (use subprocess with the project's existing pip command discovery; mirror what install-plan currently SUGGESTS).
   - On success, calls into vibecomfy/node_packs_lockfile.py to UPSERT the new entry with git_commit_sha read from the cloned repo HEAD.
   - On failure (clone error, pip error), prints a clear error and exits non-zero. Does NOT mutate the lockfile on failure.
   - Idempotent: if the repo already exists at the install path with a clean working tree, just refreshes the SHA and returns success. If dirty, refuses unless --force.

2. New command 'vibecomfy nodes ensure --template <template-id>' OR '--workflow <path>' that:
   - Calls the existing install-plan logic to identify missing packs.
   - For each missing pack, calls the new 'install' verb.
   - After all installs, optionally calls session.reload_for_nodepack_change() if a session is reachable; if not, prints clearly that the user should reload/restart their session.
   - Exits non-zero if any install fails.
   - Has --dry-run that just prints the plan (essentially aliasing install-plan for ergonomics).

3. Pin ComfyUI-QwenTTS in custom_nodes.lock by:
   - Adding the QwenTTS entry to vibecomfy/node_packs.py:KNOWN_NODE_PACKS (lookup the actual class_types it provides — at minimum 'AILab_Qwen3TTSVoiceClone' from the talking-avatar template; check the pack's repo for the full class list).
   - Running 'vibecomfy nodes install ComfyUI-QwenTTS' (or scripted equivalent at execution time) to populate the lockfile entry, OR if installing during the megaplan run is risky, just record the intended entry and document the user-action follow-up.
   - Repo URL: research the canonical ComfyUI-QwenTTS repo (likely under 'AIFSH' or similar org on GitHub — verify before committing).

4. Optional but nice-to-have: add an 'ensure_packs: bool = False' kwarg to EmbeddedSession.run(...) that calls the ensure path before executing. Default False (opt-in, no surprise installs). If True and a pack is missing, install + reload + then execute. Document explicitly that this is a convenience for dev workflows; production should pre-stage installs via 'vibecomfy nodes ensure'.

5. Tests:
   - Unit test for the install verb using a mocked subprocess (don't actually clone repos in CI). Assert: lockfile UPSERT happens on success, no mutation on failure, idempotent on existing clean clone.
   - Unit test for ensure that mocks install and asserts the dependency-resolution logic.
   - If ensure_packs kwarg is added: integration-style test (mocked) that asserts session.run with ensure_packs=True calls the install path before queue_prompt_api.

CRITICAL CONSTRAINTS:

- Backward compat: existing 'vibecomfy nodes install-plan' must keep working unchanged — install is a NEW verb, not a replacement.
- Do NOT actually clone real repos or pip-install during the megaplan execution phase — use mocks for tests. The user (or post-merge CI) can do real installs.
- Do NOT touch P1 territory beyond what's necessary: vibecomfy/commands/nodes.py and vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py are P1's; touching them to ADD new subcommands is fine, but don't refactor what P1 just shipped.
- Do NOT add hot-loading without session reload — sticking to the SD-005 / plan §'Custom Nodes — Adding On the Fly' policy: 'Hot-loading a new nodepack into an already-running HiddenSwitch executor is not the default. Adding a new pack defaults to session reload or restart.'
- Do NOT touch vibecomfy/blocks/, vibecomfy/workflow.py, vibecomfy/runtime/session.py beyond adding the optional ensure_packs kwarg surface (and only if cleanly additive).

KEY FILES:
- vibecomfy/commands/nodes.py (existing install-plan; new install + ensure subcommands here)
- vibecomfy/node_packs.py:KNOWN_NODE_PACKS (registry to extend with QwenTTS)
- vibecomfy/node_packs_lockfile.py (LockEntry, read_lockfile, write_lockfile — P1 just added these)
- vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py (existing SHA verification)
- vibecomfy/runtime/session.py (EmbeddedSession.reload_for_nodepack_change exists from P1; optionally add ensure_packs kwarg to .run())
- ready_templates/video/ltx2_3_runexx_talking_avatar_qwen_tts.py:363 (the template that uses QwenTTS)
- custom_nodes.lock (current entries: KJNodes, WanVideoWrapper)
- docs/python_composition_dsl_plan.md §'Custom Nodes' for design intent

ACCEPTANCE:
- 'vibecomfy nodes install ComfyUI-QwenTTS' (with appropriate args) installs the pack and updates the lockfile (mocked in tests; real install is user follow-up).
- 'vibecomfy nodes ensure --template video/ltx2_3_runexx_talking_avatar_qwen_tts' identifies missing packs and installs them (mocked).
- ComfyUI-QwenTTS is recorded in KNOWN_NODE_PACKS with its class types.
- Existing 'install-plan' command still works.
- All existing tests pass.
- New tests for install / ensure behavior pass with mocks.
- doctor still fail-closes on a synthetic mismatch.

Build the neutral module FIRST and resist the temptation to put any subprocess or lockfile-mutation logic inside vibecomfy/commands/nodes.py. The CLI module is print + exit-code translation only.\n\nORDERING. Strict execution order: T1 (UPSERT helper) \u2192 T2 (neutral module) \u2192 T3 (neutral module tests) \u2192 T4 (CLI wrappers + refactor install-plan) \u2192 T5 (CLI tests) \u2192 T6 (ensure_packs kwarg) \u2192 T7 (runtime tests) \u2192 T8 (QwenTTS pin + docs) \u2192 T9 (full sweep). T4 and T6 both depend on T2 but are independent of each other; T8 depends on T4 because the docs reference the new install verb.\n\nINSTALL-PLAN REFACTOR \u2014 ZERO BEHAVIOR CHANGE. The extraction of resolve logic into missing_packs_for_workflow is pure plumbing. Print formatting, exit codes, and --json shape MUST remain byte-identical. Run tests/test_cli.py::test_nodes_install_plan_suggests_pack_for_missing_class immediately after the refactor as the canary \u2014 if it fails the extraction is wrong.\n\nSUBPROCESS INJECTION. install_pack accepts `runner: Callable = subprocess.run` as a parameter. Tests pass a fake; production uses the default. Do NOT use module-global monkeypatching \u2014 it's the brittle pattern this plan explicitly avoids. The fake records call signatures and returns either CompletedProcess(stdout=..., stderr=..., returncode=0) or raises CalledProcessError, depending on which test path you're exercising.\n\nDIRTY + FORCE = NO DESTRUCTIVE OPS. The plan is explicit: --force on a dirty checkout skips the clone but does NOT run git checkout or git reset. It just UPSERTs the current HEAD. This is conservative on purpose \u2014 destructive ops require explicit user request.\n\nQWENTTS PIN MECHANICS. Run `git ls-remote https://github.com/1038lab/ComfyUI-QwenTTS.git HEAD` (read-only network, no clone, no pip \u2014 this is allowed by the no-real-clone constraint). Capture the SHA from stdout, then edit custom_nodes.lock. Pick the lower-friction format: if the file is still 2 legacy lines, append a third legacy line; if migrating to TOML is simpler given the existing P1 _cmd_nodes_lock writes TOML, do that. Both are read-compatible per read_lockfile. Verify KNOWN_NODE_PACKS at vibecomfy/node_packs.py:116 \u2014 the plan says QwenTTS is already there with AILab_Qwen3TTSVoiceClone; if it is, no code edit. If it is not, add it.\n\nENSURE_PACKS LAZY IMPORT. The import at the top of vibecomfy/runtime/session.py must NOT include vibecomfy.node_packs_install. Put the import inside the run() body, conditionally on ensure_packs=True. This prevents accidental coupling and keeps the runtime startup cost unchanged when the kwarg is not used.\n\nENSURE_PACKS RELOAD CONSERVATISM. Skip reload_for_nodepack_change when missing_packs_for_workflow returns empty. The brief and SD-005 are conservative: only reload when a pack was actually installed or refreshed.\n\nLOCKFILE NON-MUTATION ON FAILURE. This is the most important invariant in the install_pack logic. Two failure paths (clone, pip) \u2014 both must leave custom_nodes.lock untouched. The fake-runner tests for both paths are required, not optional.\n\nDEBT WATCH. The pre-existing debt items (handle hash/eq, blocks\u2192workflow circular import risk, .out(name) NotImplementedError) are tracked but UNRELATED to this PR. Don't be tempted to fix them here \u2014 this is MP-Pack-Ensure scope only.\n\nJUDGMENT CALL. The plan asks an open question about lockfile format (legacy vs TOML). The executor should pick the smaller diff: if KJNodes/WanVideoWrapper are still 2 legacy lines, append a third legacy line for QwenTTS.         Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan places _coercion_log.py under vibecomfy/blocks/ but also needs vibeworkflow.connect in vibecomfy/workflow.py to consult handle_trace_active and record events. because importing vibecomfy.blocks._coercion_log executes vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py, and that package currently imports vibeworkflow at top level, any top-level import from workflow.py to vibecomfy.blocks._coercion_log would create a circular import risk unless the implementation uses carefully lazy imports or moves the shared trace module outside the blocks package. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure: does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the proposed _coercion_log.py under vibecomfy/blocks/ is supporting infrastructure that both blocks and workflow need. given the current package dependency direction (blocks imports workflow), the safer location is a neutral module such as vibecomfy/handles.py or vibecomfy/handle_warnings.py; leaving it under blocks increases import-order fragility unless the implementation is very deliberate. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them: find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: for the block helper caller path, vibeworkflow.connect is usually called by vibecomfy.blocks._utils.connect, not by the user's block function directly. the plan's immediate-caller-frame heuristic can work if _utils.connect has a handle local named source, but it will not identify the higher-level block function unless record_raw_ref_coercion preserves or walks to the block frame rather than skipping blocks frames. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] handle-equality-hash-semantics: strict python hash/eq invariant violated for the bare-id compatibility bridge (handle('1', 0) == '1' and == '1.0' but hash('1') != hash('1.0')). (flagged 2 times across 2 plans)
- [DEBT] handle-equality-hash-semantics: hash/eq compromise is documented but not strictly satisfied for bare-id bridge. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] import-architecture: import architecture: placing shared trace state in `vibecomfy.blocks._coercion_log` risks circular imports because `workflow.py` needs the state for connect-site detection while `vibecomfy.blocks.__init__` currently imports `vibeworkflow` at package import time. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] search-for-related-code-that-handles-the-same-concept-is-the-reported-issue-a-symptom-of-something-broader: search for related code that handles the same concept. is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: the plan's record_raw_ref_coercion helper says it finds the first non-vibecomfy/blocks frame for the site. that conflicts with the stated goal that raw string refs in block code are visible: a missed handles(image="...") inside a block called by a ready template could be attributed to an allowlisted ready_templates frame and suppressed, or at minimum would not identify the block module as the source. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] untyped-raw-ref-doctor-rule-coverage: runtime detection covers handles values and connect refs but not arbitrary python dict keys outside those flows. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] wf-node-out-api-contract: non-digit slot names raise notimplementederror instead of returning a handle; diverges from brief's .out(name_or_index) literal wording. (flagged 2 times across 2 plans)
- [DEBT] wf-node-out-api-contract: same as issue_hints-1. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:

        Idea:
MP-Pack-Ensure: add the missing 'actually install' verb so vibecomfy can chain install-plan → install → reload in one command, plus pin ComfyUI-QwenTTS in the lockfile.

Today (post-P1) the discovery and verification machinery is wired but installation itself is a printed plan, not an action:
- vibecomfy/commands/nodes.py has _cmd_nodes_install_plan() which scans a workflow, finds missing class_types, looks them up in KNOWN_NODE_PACKS, and PRINTS the suggested packs.
- vibecomfy/node_packs.py:KNOWN_NODE_PACKS is the static registry mapping class_type → CustomNodePack(name, repo, classes, pip_packages).
- vibecomfy/node_packs_lockfile.py reads/writes custom_nodes.lock with git_commit_sha pins (added in P1).
- vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py fail-closes on SHA drift before session.start() (added in P1).
- EmbeddedSession.reload_for_nodepack_change() is the post-install reload verb (added in P1).

The gap: no command actually performs the install. User has to copy the printed git clone + pip install commands and run them by hand. This is also why ComfyUI-QwenTTS is referenced in ready_templates/video/ltx2_3_runexx_talking_avatar_qwen_tts.py:363 but is not pinned in custom_nodes.lock — the workflow worked with manual install but never went through the lockfile path.

DELIVERABLES:

1. New command 'vibecomfy nodes install <pack-name>' OR '--repo <url>' that:
   - Clones the repo into the local custom_nodes directory (search the project for the canonical install path; check vibecomfy/runtime/session.py and existing scripts to find where Comfy expects custom nodes — likely a 'custom_nodes/' dir).
   - Pip-installs the pack's pip_packages from KNOWN_NODE_PACKS (use subprocess with the project's existing pip command discovery; mirror what install-plan currently SUGGESTS).
   - On success, calls into vibecomfy/node_packs_lockfile.py to UPSERT the new entry with git_commit_sha read from the cloned repo HEAD.
   - On failure (clone error, pip error), prints a clear error and exits non-zero. Does NOT mutate the lockfile on failure.
   - Idempotent: if the repo already exists at the install path with a clean working tree, just refreshes the SHA and returns success. If dirty, refuses unless --force.

2. New command 'vibecomfy nodes ensure --template <template-id>' OR '--workflow <path>' that:
   - Calls the existing install-plan logic to identify missing packs.
   - For each missing pack, calls the new 'install' verb.
   - After all installs, optionally calls session.reload_for_nodepack_change() if a session is reachable; if not, prints clearly that the user should reload/restart their session.
   - Exits non-zero if any install fails.
   - Has --dry-run that just prints the plan (essentially aliasing install-plan for ergonomics).

3. Pin ComfyUI-QwenTTS in custom_nodes.lock by:
   - Adding the QwenTTS entry to vibecomfy/node_packs.py:KNOWN_NODE_PACKS (lookup the actual class_types it provides — at minimum 'AILab_Qwen3TTSVoiceClone' from the talking-avatar template; check the pack's repo for the full class list).
   - Running 'vibecomfy nodes install ComfyUI-QwenTTS' (or scripted equivalent at execution time) to populate the lockfile entry, OR if installing during the megaplan run is risky, just record the intended entry and document the user-action follow-up.
   - Repo URL: research the canonical ComfyUI-QwenTTS repo (likely under 'AIFSH' or similar org on GitHub — verify before committing).

4. Optional but nice-to-have: add an 'ensure_packs: bool = False' kwarg to EmbeddedSession.run(...) that calls the ensure path before executing. Default False (opt-in, no surprise installs). If True and a pack is missing, install + reload + then execute. Document explicitly that this is a convenience for dev workflows; production should pre-stage installs via 'vibecomfy nodes ensure'.

5. Tests:
   - Unit test for the install verb using a mocked subprocess (don't actually clone repos in CI). Assert: lockfile UPSERT happens on success, no mutation on failure, idempotent on existing clean clone.
   - Unit test for ensure that mocks install and asserts the dependency-resolution logic.
   - If ensure_packs kwarg is added: integration-style test (mocked) that asserts session.run with ensure_packs=True calls the install path before queue_prompt_api.

CRITICAL CONSTRAINTS:

- Backward compat: existing 'vibecomfy nodes install-plan' must keep working unchanged — install is a NEW verb, not a replacement.
- Do NOT actually clone real repos or pip-install during the megaplan execution phase — use mocks for tests. The user (or post-merge CI) can do real installs.
- Do NOT touch P1 territory beyond what's necessary: vibecomfy/commands/nodes.py and vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py are P1's; touching them to ADD new subcommands is fine, but don't refactor what P1 just shipped.
- Do NOT add hot-loading without session reload — sticking to the SD-005 / plan §'Custom Nodes — Adding On the Fly' policy: 'Hot-loading a new nodepack into an already-running HiddenSwitch executor is not the default. Adding a new pack defaults to session reload or restart.'
- Do NOT touch vibecomfy/blocks/, vibecomfy/workflow.py, vibecomfy/runtime/session.py beyond adding the optional ensure_packs kwarg surface (and only if cleanly additive).

KEY FILES:
- vibecomfy/commands/nodes.py (existing install-plan; new install + ensure subcommands here)
- vibecomfy/node_packs.py:KNOWN_NODE_PACKS (registry to extend with QwenTTS)
- vibecomfy/node_packs_lockfile.py (LockEntry, read_lockfile, write_lockfile — P1 just added these)
- vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py (existing SHA verification)
- vibecomfy/runtime/session.py (EmbeddedSession.reload_for_nodepack_change exists from P1; optionally add ensure_packs kwarg to .run())
- ready_templates/video/ltx2_3_runexx_talking_avatar_qwen_tts.py:363 (the template that uses QwenTTS)
- custom_nodes.lock (current entries: KJNodes, WanVideoWrapper)
- docs/python_composition_dsl_plan.md §'Custom Nodes' for design intent

ACCEPTANCE:
- 'vibecomfy nodes install ComfyUI-QwenTTS' (with appropriate args) installs the pack and updates the lockfile (mocked in tests; real install is user follow-up).
- 'vibecomfy nodes ensure --template video/ltx2_3_runexx_talking_avatar_qwen_tts' identifies missing packs and installs them (mocked).
- ComfyUI-QwenTTS is recorded in KNOWN_NODE_PACKS with its class types.
- Existing 'install-plan' command still works.
- All existing tests pass.
- New tests for install / ensure behavior pass with mocks.
- doctor still fail-closes on a synthetic mismatch.

Idempotent path: dir exists + clean porcelain \u2192 rev-parse HEAD, UPSERT, status='refreshed'; dir exists + dirty + no force \u2192 status='skipped_dirty', NO UPSERT; dir exists + dirty + force \u2192 skip clone, UPSERT current HEAD, status='refreshed' (no destructive git ops). Clone path: dir absent \u2192 runner(['git','clone',repo_url,str(install_dir)], check=True, capture_output=True, text=True); on CalledProcessError return status='failed' with stderr in error and NO UPSERT; if catalog pip_packages non-empty, runner([sys.executable,'-m','pip','install',*pip_packages], check=True, ...); on pip failure status='failed' and NO UPSERT; success \u2192 rev-parse HEAD, upsert_lockfile_entry(LockEntry(name, sha, repo_url)), status='installed'. CRITICAL: no module-level imports from vibecomfy.commands.*; no print/sys.exit; raise ValueError for programmer errors (neither name nor repo given).   "meta_commentary": "EXECUTION GUIDANCE\n\nArchitecture is the load-bearing decision here. The plan's two flagged concerns were both about layering: FLAG-001 (QwenTTS is now a real lockfile pin, not a docs note) and FLAG-002 (the runtime kwarg must depend on a neutral module, never on CLI command wiring). Build the neutral module FIRST and resist the temptation to put any subprocess or lockfile-mutation logic inside vibecomfy/commands/nodes.py. The CLI module is print + exit-code translation only.\n\nORDERING. Strict execution order: T1 (UPSERT helper) \u2192 T2 (neutral module) \u2192 T3 (neutral module tests) \u2192 T4 (CLI wrappers + refactor install-plan) \u2192 T5 (CLI tests) \u2192 T6 (ensure_packs kwarg) \u2192 T7 (runtime tests) \u2192 T8 (QwenTTS pin + docs) \u2192 T9 (full sweep). T4 and T6 both depend on T2 but are independent of each other; T8 depends on T4 because the docs reference the new install verb.\n\nINSTALL-PLAN REFACTOR \u2014 ZERO BEHAVIOR CHANGE. The extraction of resolve logic into missing_packs_for_workflow is pure plumbing. Print formatting, exit codes, and --json shape MUST remain byte-identical. Run tests/test_cli.py::test_nodes_install_plan_suggests_pack_for_missing_class immediately after the refactor as the canary \u2014 if it fails the extraction is wrong.\n\nSUBPROCESS INJECTION. install_pack accepts `runner: Callable = subprocess.run` as a parameter. Tests pass a fake; production uses the default. Do NOT use module-global monkeypatching \u2014 it's the brittle pattern this plan explicitly avoids. The fake records call signatures and returns either CompletedProcess(stdout=..., stderr=..., returncode=0) or raises CalledProcessError, depending on which test path you're exercising.\n\nDIRTY + FORCE = NO DESTRUCTIVE OPS. The plan is explicit: --force on a dirty checkout skips the clone but does NOT run git checkout or git reset. It just UPSERTs the current HEAD. This is conservative on purpose \u2014 destructive ops require explicit user request.\n\nQWENTTS PIN MECHANICS. Run `git ls-remote https://github.com/1038lab/ComfyUI-QwenTTS.git HEAD` (read-only network, no clone, no pip \u2014 this is allowed by the no-real-clone constraint). Capture the SHA from stdout, then edit custom_nodes.lock. Pick the lower-friction format: if the file is still 2 legacy lines, append a third legacy line; if migrating to TOML is simpler given the existing P1 _cmd_nodes_lock writes TOML, do that. Both are read-compatible per read_lockfile. Verify KNOWN_NODE_PACKS at vibecomfy/node_packs.py:116 \u2014 the plan says QwenTTS is already there with AILab_Qwen3TTSVoiceClone; if it is, no code edit. If it is not, add it.\n\nENSURE_PACKS LAZY IMPORT. The import at the top of vibecomfy/runtime/session.py must NOT include vibecomfy.node_packs_install. Put the import inside the run() body, conditionally on ensure_packs=True. This prevents accidental coupling and keeps the runtime startup cost unchanged when the kwarg is not used.\n\nENSURE_PACKS RELOAD CONSERVATISM. Skip reload_for_nodepack_change when missing_packs_for_workflow returns empty. The brief and SD-005 are conservative: only reload when a pack was actually installed or refreshed.\n\nLOCKFILE NON-MUTATION ON FAILURE. This is the most important invariant in the install_pack logic. Two failure paths (clone, pip) \u2014 both must leave custom_nodes.lock untouched. The fake-runner tests for both paths are required, not optional.\n\nDEBT WATCH. The pre-existing debt items (handle hash/eq, blocks\u2192workflow circular import risk, .out(name) NotImplementedError) are tracked but UNRELATED to this PR. Don't be tempted to fix them here \u2014 this is MP-Pack-Ensure scope only.\n\nJUDGMENT CALL. The plan asks an open question about lockfile format (legacy vs TOML). The executor should pick the smaller diff: if KJNodes/WanVideoWrapper are still 2 legacy lines, append a third legacy line for QwenTTS. The migration to TOML can happen organically when a user runs `vibecomfy nodes install` for the first time.",

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan places _coercion_log.py under vibecomfy/blocks/ but also needs vibeworkflow.connect in vibecomfy/workflow.py to consult handle_trace_active and record events. because importing vibecomfy.blocks._coercion_log executes vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py, and that package currently imports vibeworkflow at top level, any top-level import from workflow.py to vibecomfy.blocks._coercion_log would create a circular import risk unless the implementation uses carefully lazy imports or moves the shared trace module outside the blocks package. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure: does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the proposed _coercion_log.py under vibecomfy/blocks/ is supporting infrastructure that both blocks and workflow need. given the current package dependency direction (blocks imports workflow), the safer location is a neutral module such as vibecomfy/handles.py or vibecomfy/handle_warnings.py; leaving it under blocks increases import-order fragility unless the implementation is very deliberate. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them: find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: for the block helper caller path, vibeworkflow.connect is usually called by vibecomfy.blocks._utils.connect, not by the user's block function directly. the plan's immediate-caller-frame heuristic can work if _utils.connect has a handle local named source, but it will not identify the higher-level block function unless record_raw_ref_coercion preserves or walks to the block frame rather than skipping blocks frames. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] handle-equality-hash-semantics: strict python hash/eq invariant violated for the bare-id compatibility bridge (handle('1', 0) == '1' and == '1.0' but hash('1') != hash('1.0')). (flagged 2 times across 2 plans)
- [DEBT] handle-equality-hash-semantics: hash/eq compromise is documented but not strictly satisfied for bare-id bridge. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] import-architecture: import architecture: placing shared trace state in `vibecomfy.blocks._coercion_log` risks circular imports because `workflow.py` needs the state for connect-site detection while `vibecomfy.blocks.__init__` currently imports `vibeworkflow` at package import time. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] search-for-related-code-that-handles-the-same-concept-is-the-reported-issue-a-symptom-of-something-broader: search for related code that handles the same concept. is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: the plan's record_raw_ref_coercion helper says it finds the first non-vibecomfy/blocks frame for the site. that conflicts with the stated goal that raw string refs in block code are visible: a missed handles(image="...") inside a block called by a ready template could be attributed to an allowlisted ready_templates frame and suppressed, or at minimum would not identify the block module as the source. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] untyped-raw-ref-doctor-rule-coverage: runtime detection covers handles values and connect refs but not arbitrary python dict keys outside those flows. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

- [DEBT] wf-node-out-api-contract: same as issue_hints-1. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

        Idea:
MP-Pack-Ensure: add the missing 'actually install' verb so vibecomfy can chain install-plan → install → reload in one command, plus pin ComfyUI-QwenTTS in the lockfile.

Today (post-P1) the discovery and verification machinery is wired but installation itself is a printed plan, not an action:
- vibecomfy/commands/nodes.py has _cmd_nodes_install_plan() which scans a workflow, finds missing class_types, looks them up in KNOWN_NODE_PACKS, and PRINTS the suggested packs.
- vibecomfy/node_packs.py:KNOWN_NODE_PACKS is the static registry mapping class_type → CustomNodePack(name, repo, classes, pip_packages).
- vibecomfy/node_packs_lockfile.py reads/writes custom_nodes.lock with git_commit_sha pins (added in P1).
- vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py fail-closes on SHA drift before session.start() (added in P1).
- EmbeddedSession.reload_for_nodepack_change() is the post-install reload verb (added in P1).

The gap: no command actually performs the install. User has to copy the printed git clone + pip install commands and run them by hand. This is also why ComfyUI-QwenTTS is referenced in ready_templates/video/ltx2_3_runexx_talking_avatar_qwen_tts.py:363 but is not pinned in custom_nodes.lock — the workflow worked with manual install but never went through the lockfile path.

DELIVERABLES:

1. New command 'vibecomfy nodes install <pack-name>' OR '--repo <url>' that:
   - Clones the repo into the local custom_nodes directory (search the project for the canonical install path; check vibecomfy/runtime/session.py and existing scripts to find where Comfy expects custom nodes — likely a 'custom_nodes/' dir).
   - Pip-installs the pack's pip_packages from KNOWN_NODE_PACKS (use subprocess with the project's existing pip command discovery; mirror what install-plan currently SUGGESTS).
   - On success, calls into vibecomfy/node_packs_lockfile.py to UPSERT the new entry with git_commit_sha read from the cloned repo HEAD.
   - On failure (clone error, pip error), prints a clear error and exits non-zero. Does NOT mutate the lockfile on failure.
   - Idempotent: if the repo already exists at the install path with a clean working tree, just refreshes the SHA and returns success. If dirty, refuses unless --force.

2. New command 'vibecomfy nodes ensure --template <template-id>' OR '--workflow <path>' that:
   - Calls the existing install-plan logic to identify missing packs.
   - For each missing pack, calls the new 'install' verb.
   - After all installs, optionally calls session.reload_for_nodepack_change() if a session is reachable; if not, prints clearly that the user should reload/restart their session.
   - Exits non-zero if any install fails.
   - Has --dry-run that just prints the plan (essentially aliasing install-plan for ergonomics).

3. Pin ComfyUI-QwenTTS in custom_nodes.lock by:
   - Adding the QwenTTS entry to vibecomfy/node_packs.py:KNOWN_NODE_PACKS (lookup the actual class_types it provides — at minimum 'AILab_Qwen3TTSVoiceClone' from the talking-avatar template; check the pack's repo for the full class list).
   - Running 'vibecomfy nodes install ComfyUI-QwenTTS' (or scripted equivalent at execution time) to populate the lockfile entry, OR if installing during the megaplan run is risky, just record the intended entry and document the user-action follow-up.
   - Repo URL: research the canonical ComfyUI-QwenTTS repo (likely under 'AIFSH' or similar org on GitHub — verify before committing).

4. Optional but nice-to-have: add an 'ensure_packs: bool = False' kwarg to EmbeddedSession.run(...) that calls the ensure path before executing. Default False (opt-in, no surprise installs). If True and a pack is missing, install + reload + then execute. Document explicitly that this is a convenience for dev workflows; production should pre-stage installs via 'vibecomfy nodes ensure'.

5. Tests:
   - Unit test for the install verb using a mocked subprocess (don't actually clone repos in CI). Assert: lockfile UPSERT happens on success, no mutation on failure, idempotent on existing clean clone.
   - Unit test for ensure that mocks install and asserts the dependency-resolution logic.
   - If ensure_packs kwarg is added: integration-style test (mocked) that asserts session.run with ensure_packs=True calls the install path before queue_prompt_api.

  "meta_commentary": "EXECUTION GUIDANCE\n\nArchitecture is the load-bearing decision here. The plan's two flagged concerns were both about layering: FLAG-001 (QwenTTS is now a real lockfile pin, not a docs note) and FLAG-002 (the runtime kwarg must depend on a neutral module, never on CLI command wiring). Build the neutral module FIRST and resist the temptation to put any subprocess or lockfile-mutation logic inside vibecomfy/commands/nodes.py. The CLI module is print + exit-code translation only.\n\nORDERING. Strict execution order: T1 (UPSERT helper) \u2192 T2 (neutral module) \u2192 T3 (neutral module tests) \u2192 T4 (CLI wrappers + refactor install-plan) \u2192 T5 (CLI tests) \u2192 T6 (ensure_packs kwarg) \u2192 T7 (runtime tests) \u2192 T8 (QwenTTS pin + docs) \u2192 T9 (full sweep). T4 and T6 both depend on T2 but are independent of each other; T8 depends on T4 because the docs reference the new install verb.\n\nINSTALL-PLAN REFACTOR \u2014 ZERO BEHAVIOR CHANGE. The extraction of resolve logic into missing_packs_for_workflow is pure plumbing. Print formatting, exit codes, and --json shape MUST remain byte-identical. Run tests/test_cli.py::test_nodes_install_plan_suggests_pack_for_missing_class immediately after the refactor as the canary \u2014 if it fails the extraction is wrong.\n\nSUBPROCESS INJECTION. install_pack accepts `runner: Callable = subprocess.run` as a parameter. Tests pass a fake; production uses the default. Do NOT use module-global monkeypatching \u2014 it's the brittle pattern this plan explicitly avoids. The fake records call signatures and returns either CompletedProcess(stdout=..., stderr=..., returncode=0) or raises CalledProcessError, depending on which test path you're exercising.\n\nDIRTY + FORCE = NO DESTRUCTIVE OPS. The plan is explicit: --force on a dirty checkout skips the clone but does NOT run git checkout or git reset. It just UPSERTs the current HEAD. This is conservative on purpose \u2014 destructive ops require explicit user request.\n\nQWENTTS PIN MECHANICS. Run `git ls-remote https://github.com/1038lab/ComfyUI-QwenTTS.git HEAD` (read-only network, no clone, no pip \u2014 this is allowed by the no-real-clone constraint). Capture the SHA from stdout, then edit custom_nodes.lock. Pick the lower-friction format: if the file is still 2 legacy lines, append a third legacy line; if migrating to TOML is simpler given the existing P1 _cmd_nodes_lock writes TOML, do that. Both are read-compatible per read_lockfile. Verify KNOWN_NODE_PACKS at vibecomfy/node_packs.py:116 \u2014 the plan says QwenTTS is already there with AILab_Qwen3TTSVoiceClone; if it is, no code edit. If it is not, add it.\n\nENSURE_PACKS LAZY IMPORT. The import at the top of vibecomfy/runtime/session.py must NOT include vibecomfy.node_packs_install. Put the import inside the run() body, conditionally on ensure_packs=True. This prevents accidental coupling and keeps the runtime startup cost unchanged when the kwarg is not used.\n\nENSURE_PACKS RELOAD CONSERVATISM. Skip reload_for_nodepack_change when missing_packs_for_workflow returns empty. The brief and SD-005 are conservative: only reload when a pack was actually installed or refreshed.\n\nLOCKFILE NON-MUTATION ON FAILURE. This is the most important invariant in the install_pack logic. Two failure paths (clone, pip) \u2014 both must leave custom_nodes.lock untouched. The fake-runner tests for both paths are required, not optional.\n\nDEBT WATCH. The pre-existing debt items (handle hash/eq, blocks\u2192workflow circular import risk, .out(name) NotImplementedError) are tracked but UNRELATED to this PR. Don't be tempted to fix them here \u2014 this is MP-Pack-Ensure scope only.\n\nJUDGMENT CALL. The plan asks an open question about lockfile format (legacy vs TOML). The executor should pick the smaller diff: if KJNodes/WanVideoWrapper are still 2 legacy lines, append a third legacy line for QwenTTS. The migration to TOML can happen organically when a user runs `vibecomfy nodes install` for the first time.",

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan places _coercion_log.py under vibecomfy/blocks/ but also needs vibeworkflow.connect in vibecomfy/workflow.py to consult handle_trace_active and record events. because importing vibecomfy.blocks._coercion_log executes vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py, and that package currently imports vibeworkflow at top level, any top-level import from workflow.py to vibecomfy.blocks._coercion_log would create a circular import risk unless the implementation uses carefully lazy imports or moves the shared trace module outside the blocks package. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure: does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the proposed _coercion_log.py under vibecomfy/blocks/ is supporting infrastructure that both blocks and workflow need. given the current package dependency direction (blocks imports workflow), the safer location is a neutral module such as vibecomfy/handles.py or vibecomfy/handle_warnings.py; leaving it under blocks increases import-order fragility unless the implementation is very deliberate. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them: find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: for the block helper caller path, vibeworkflow.connect is usually called by vibecomfy.blocks._utils.connect, not by the user's block function directly. the plan's immediate-caller-frame heuristic can work if _utils.connect has a handle local named source, but it will not identify the higher-level block function unless record_raw_ref_coercion preserves or walks to the block frame rather than skipping blocks frames. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] handle-equality-hash-semantics: strict python hash/eq invariant violated for the bare-id compatibility bridge (handle('1', 0) == '1' and == '1.0' but hash('1') != hash('1.0')). (flagged 2 times across 2 plans)
- [DEBT] handle-equality-hash-semantics: hash/eq compromise is documented but not strictly satisfied for bare-id bridge. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] import-architecture: import architecture: placing shared trace state in `vibecomfy.blocks._coercion_log` risks circular imports because `workflow.py` needs the state for connect-site detection while `vibecomfy.blocks.__init__` currently imports `vibeworkflow` at package import time. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] search-for-related-code-that-handles-the-same-concept-is-the-reported-issue-a-symptom-of-something-broader: search for related code that handles the same concept. is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: the plan's record_raw_ref_coercion helper says it finds the first non-vibecomfy/blocks frame for the site. that conflicts with the stated goal that raw string refs in block code are visible: a missed handles(image="...") inside a block called by a ready template could be attributed to an allowlisted ready_templates frame and suppressed, or at minimum would not identify the block module as the source. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] untyped-raw-ref-doctor-rule-coverage: runtime detection covers handles values and connect refs but not arbitrary python dict keys outside those flows. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] wf-node-out-api-contract: non-digit slot names raise notimplementederror instead of returning a handle; diverges from brief's .out(name_or_index) literal wording. (flagged 2 times across 2 plans)
- [DEBT] wf-node-out-api-contract: same as issue_hints-1. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy





        Idea:
MP-Pack-Ensure: add the missing 'actually install' verb so vibecomfy can chain install-plan → install → reload in one command, plus pin ComfyUI-QwenTTS in the lockfile.

Today (post-P1) the discovery and verification machinery is wired but installation itself is a printed plan, not an action:
- vibecomfy/commands/nodes.py has _cmd_nodes_install_plan() which scans a workflow, finds missing class_types, looks them up in KNOWN_NODE_PACKS, and PRINTS the suggested packs.
- vibecomfy/node_packs.py:KNOWN_NODE_PACKS is the static registry mapping class_type → CustomNodePack(name, repo, classes, pip_packages).
- vibecomfy/node_packs_lockfile.py reads/writes custom_nodes.lock with git_commit_sha pins (added in P1).
- vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py fail-closes on SHA drift before session.start() (added in P1).
- EmbeddedSession.reload_for_nodepack_change() is the post-install reload verb (added in P1).

The gap: no command actually performs the install. User has to copy the printed git clone + pip install commands and run them by hand. This is also why ComfyUI-QwenTTS is referenced in ready_templates/video/ltx2_3_runexx_talking_avatar_qwen_tts.py:363 but is not pinned in custom_nodes.lock — the workflow worked with manual install but never went through the lockfile path.

DELIVERABLES:

1. New command 'vibecomfy nodes install <pack-name>' OR '--repo <url>' that:
   - Clones the repo into the local custom_nodes directory (search the project for the canonical install path; check vibecomfy/runtime/session.py and existing scripts to find where Comfy expects custom nodes — likely a 'custom_nodes/' dir).
   - Pip-installs the pack's pip_packages from KNOWN_NODE_PACKS (use subprocess with the project's existing pip command discovery; mirror what install-plan currently SUGGESTS).
   - On success, calls into vibecomfy/node_packs_lockfile.py to UPSERT the new entry with git_commit_sha read from the cloned repo HEAD.
   - On failure (clone error, pip error), prints a clear error and exits non-zero. Does NOT mutate the lockfile on failure.
   - Idempotent: if the repo already exists at the install path with a clean working tree, just refreshes the SHA and returns success. If dirty, refuses unless --force.

2. New command 'vibecomfy nodes ensure --template <template-id>' OR '--workflow <path>' that:
   - Calls the existing install-plan logic to identify missing packs.
   - For each missing pack, calls the new 'install' verb.
   - After all installs, optionally calls session.reload_for_nodepack_change() if a session is reachable; if not, prints clearly that the user should reload/restart their session.
   - Exits non-zero if any install fails.
   - Has --dry-run that just prints the plan (essentially aliasing install-plan for ergonomics).

3. Pin ComfyUI-QwenTTS in custom_nodes.lock by:
   - Adding the QwenTTS entry to vibecomfy/node_packs.py:KNOWN_NODE_PACKS (lookup the actual class_types it provides — at minimum 'AILab_Qwen3TTSVoiceClone' from the talking-avatar template; check the pack's repo for the full class list).
   - Running 'vibecomfy nodes install ComfyUI-QwenTTS' (or scripted equivalent at execution time) to populate the lockfile entry, OR if installing during the megaplan run is risky, just record the intended entry and document the user-action follow-up.
   - Repo URL: research the canonical ComfyUI-QwenTTS repo (likely under 'AIFSH' or similar org on GitHub — verify before committing).

4. Optional but nice-to-have: add an 'ensure_packs: bool = False' kwarg to EmbeddedSession.run(...) that calls the ensure path before executing. Default False (opt-in, no surprise installs). If True and a pack is missing, install + reload + then execute. Document explicitly that this is a convenience for dev workflows; production should pre-stage installs via 'vibecomfy nodes ensure'.

5. Tests:
   - Unit test for the install verb using a mocked subprocess (don't actually clone repos in CI). Assert: lockfile UPSERT happens on success, no mutation on failure, idempotent on existing clean clone.
   - Unit test for ensure that mocks install and asserts the dependency-resolution logic.
   - If ensure_packs kwarg is added: integration-style test (mocked) that asserts session.run with ensure_packs=True calls the install path before queue_prompt_api.

CRITICAL CONSTRAINTS:

- Backward compat: existing 'vibecomfy nodes install-plan' must keep working unchanged — install is a NEW verb, not a replacement.
- Do NOT actually clone real repos or pip-install during the megaplan execution phase — use mocks for tests. The user (or post-merge CI) can do real installs.
- Do NOT touch P1 territory beyond what's necessary: vibecomfy/commands/nodes.py and vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py are P1's; touching them to ADD new subcommands is fine, but don't refactor what P1 just shipped.
- Do NOT add hot-loading without session reload — sticking to the SD-005 / plan §'Custom Nodes — Adding On the Fly' policy: 'Hot-loading a new nodepack into an already-running HiddenSwitch executor is not the default. Adding a new pack defaults to session reload or restart.'
- Do NOT touch vibecomfy/blocks/, vibecomfy/workflow.py, vibecomfy/runtime/session.py beyond adding the optional ensure_packs kwarg surface (and only if cleanly additive).

KEY FILES:
- vibecomfy/commands/nodes.py (existing install-plan; new install + ensure subcommands here)
- vibecomfy/node_packs.py:KNOWN_NODE_PACKS (registry to extend with QwenTTS)
- vibecomfy/node_packs_lockfile.py (LockEntry, read_lockfile, write_lockfile — P1 just added these)
- vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py (existing SHA verification)
- vibecomfy/runtime/session.py (EmbeddedSession.reload_for_nodepack_change exists from P1; optionally add ensure_packs kwarg to .run())
- ready_templates/video/ltx2_3_runexx_talking_avatar_qwen_tts.py:363 (the template that uses QwenTTS)
- custom_nodes.lock (current entries: KJNodes, WanVideoWrapper)
- docs/python_composition_dsl_plan.md §'Custom Nodes' for design intent

ACCEPTANCE:
- 'vibecomfy nodes install ComfyUI-QwenTTS' (with appropriate args) installs the pack and updates the lockfile (mocked in tests; real install is user follow-up).
- 'vibecomfy nodes ensure --template video/ltx2_3_runexx_talking_avatar_qwen_tts' identifies missing packs and installs them (mocked).
- ComfyUI-QwenTTS is recorded in KNOWN_NODE_PACKS with its class types.
- Existing 'install-plan' command still works.
- All existing tests pass.
- New tests for install / ensure behavior pass with mocks.
- doctor still fail-closes on a synthetic mismatch.

  "meta_commentary": "EXECUTION GUIDANCE\n\nArchitecture is the load-bearing decision here. The plan's two flagged concerns were both about layering: FLAG-001 (QwenTTS is now a real lockfile pin, not a docs note) and FLAG-002 (the runtime kwarg must depend on a neutral module, never on CLI command wiring). Build the neutral module FIRST and resist the temptation to put any subprocess or lockfile-mutation logic inside vibecomfy/commands/nodes.py. The CLI module is print + exit-code translation only.\n\nORDERING. Strict execution order: T1 (UPSERT helper) \u2192 T2 (neutral module) \u2192 T3 (neutral module tests) \u2192 T4 (CLI wrappers + refactor install-plan) \u2192 T5 (CLI tests) \u2192 T6 (ensure_packs kwarg) \u2192 T7 (runtime tests) \u2192 T8 (QwenTTS pin + docs) \u2192 T9 (full sweep). T4 and T6 both depend on T2 but are independent of each other; T8 depends on T4 because the docs reference the new install verb.\n\nINSTALL-PLAN REFACTOR \u2014 ZERO BEHAVIOR CHANGE. The extraction of resolve logic into missing_packs_for_workflow is pure plumbing. Print formatting, exit codes, and --json shape MUST remain byte-identical. Run tests/test_cli.py::test_nodes_install_plan_suggests_pack_for_missing_class immediately after the refactor as the canary \u2014 if it fails the extraction is wrong.\n\nSUBPROCESS INJECTION. install_pack accepts `runner: Callable = subprocess.run` as a parameter. Tests pass a fake; production uses the default. Do NOT use module-global monkeypatching \u2014 it's the brittle pattern this plan explicitly avoids. The fake records call signatures and returns either CompletedProcess(stdout=..., stderr=..., returncode=0) or raises CalledProcessError, depending on which test path you're exercising.\n\nDIRTY + FORCE = NO DESTRUCTIVE OPS. The plan is explicit: --force on a dirty checkout skips the clone but does NOT run git checkout or git reset. It just UPSERTs the current HEAD. This is conservative on purpose \u2014 destructive ops require explicit user request.\n\nQWENTTS PIN MECHANICS. Run `git ls-remote https://github.com/1038lab/ComfyUI-QwenTTS.git HEAD` (read-only network, no clone, no pip \u2014 this is allowed by the no-real-clone constraint). Capture the SHA from stdout, then edit custom_nodes.lock. Pick the lower-friction format: if the file is still 2 legacy lines, append a third legacy line; if migrating to TOML is simpler given the existing P1 _cmd_nodes_lock writes TOML, do that. Both are read-compatible per read_lockfile. Verify KNOWN_NODE_PACKS at vibecomfy/node_packs.py:116 \u2014 the plan says QwenTTS is already there with AILab_Qwen3TTSVoiceClone; if it is, no code edit.
